- **user-eyes-tester**: Use this agent to test a live UI as an ordinary, non-technical user and report UI/UX feedback. Give it a URL and a task in the plain words a user would say (e.g. 'I want to find a dark spot near Tallinn'). It looks at the page like a first-time visitor, finds the way to the result as fast as it can, and records time and steps to the next useful step plus every point of confusion. Run several instances in parallel on the same URL and task, then compare the reports (compare mode). Never reads source code, specs or the DOM to decide what to do; never signs in, pays or submits real data. - **user-perspective-reviewer**: ユーザー目線でテスト成果物を評価するレビュアー。test-review の設計文脈（実利用シナリオ妥当性・UAT 観点・業務フロー網羅の評価）と結果文脈（検出欠陥のユーザー影響度・業務継続可否の評価）の両方から起動される。技術的網羅性・自動化適合性・欠陥の原因分類・エビデンスの実在監査は対象外。 ([dmajima/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dmajima/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/deep-test/agents/user-perspective-reviewer.md))
- **user-preferences**: Status: current. Portable preferences the user stated in earlier sessions, for any agent or machine (local, cloud, a new PC). Machine-specific setup, access details and personal notes stay out of the repository. Where a rule is already codified elsewhere, this page links to it instead of repeating it. ([lacucaracha421/chatgpt](https://github.com/lacucaracha421/chatgpt/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/user-preferences.md))
- **user-pressure-reviewer**: Reviews code for behavior under hostile or pathological user input patterns: API hammering (rate limits, replay), repeated UI clicks (double-submit, race), large inputs (size validation, parser DoS), malformed inputs (fuzz resistance), session abuse (long-lived tokens, concurrent sessions). Use PROACTIVELY for anything user-facing or with a public surface. Often spawned in parallel by /audit skill alongside other axis reviewers. ([sboutet06/coding-agent-working-rules](https://github.com/sboutet06/coding-agent-working-rules/blob/HEAD/agents/user-pressure-reviewer.md))
- **user-problems-researcher**: **Model tier:** standard **Background:** true **Purpose:** Researches jobs-to-be-done, user pains, and behavioral evidence for the topic via mandatory web search. Saves findings to `.codevoyant/explore/{SLUG}/research/user-problems.md`. ([cloudvoyant/codevoyant](https://github.com/cloudvoyant/codevoyant/blob/HEAD/skills/pm/agents/user-problems-researcher.md))
- **user-profile**: No user profile configured yet. The kernel will learn preferences over time. ([HamedMP/matrix-os](https://github.com/HamedMP/matrix-os/blob/HEAD/home/agents/user-profile.md) · ★89)

UI 가독성, 키 시퀀스 부담, 다국어 정합성, 도움말 친절도, 트러블슈팅 시나리오 재현. 흡수: 13개 컴포넌트별 골든패스+엣지케이스+회귀 시나리오 설계, 사용자가 따라할 수 있는 수동 테스트 체크리스트 작성(manual-test-planner). PASS/WARN/FAIL. - **user-researcher**: User researcher. Use before a feature is specified, to find out whether a real problem exists and for whom. - **user-researcher**: AI agent for customer discovery and validation. - **user-researcher**: devteam research team. Writes a sourced user-research report. ([Cphyr/devteam](https://github.com/Cphyr/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/user-researcher.md))
- **user-researcher**: Player research — plans and runs moderated playtests with real players, observes without leading, and SYNTHESIZES the findings into what players actually experienced. The qualitative player signal behind 'found the fun'. Distinct from creative-review (expert on-pitch judgement) and analytics (behavioural telemetry) — this is what watching real people reveals. Use to design a playtest, run a session, or turn raw sessions into prioritised findings. Skip when the question is expert opinion or a number, not lived player experience. ([ddunford/AgentGame](https://github.com/ddunford/AgentGame/blob/HEAD/agents/user-researcher.md))

- **user-researcher**: Talking to actual humans about Sift. - **user-review-fix-plan-writer**: Reads the user-review file for the branch, verifies each numbered observation against the current state of the code, and writes a split fix plan — a thin index plus one self-contained per-finding file — with Must Fix / Should Fix / Nice to Have / out-of-scope-or-invalid classification. Used in the user-review fix-plan writing flow, and distinct from `branch-reviewer`, which does end-of-branch diff review. - **user-rules**: - **Always install software with apt.** Never use snap, flatpak, AppImages, or manual downloads to install software on this machine.
